````bash
  local cfg=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml existing list key e line
  [[ $(grep -c '^\[\[runners\]\]' "$cfg") -eq 1 ]] || die "expected exactly one [[runners]] block in $cfg"
  existing=$(grep -E '^  environment = \[' "$cfg" | sed -E 's/^  environment = \[(.*)\]$/\1/' || true)
  list="$existing"
  for e in "$@"; do
    key="${e%%=*}"
    if grep -q "\"${key}=" <<<"$list"; then
      list=$(sed -E "s|\"${key}=[^\"]*\"|\"${e}\"|" <<<"$list")
    else
      list="${list:+${list}, }\"${e}\""
    fi
  done
  line="  environment = [${list}]"
  if grep -qE '^  environment = \[' "$cfg"; then
    sed -i -E "s|^  environment = \[.*\]$|${line}|" "$cfg"
  else
    sed -i -E "0,/^  executor = .*/s||${line}\n&|" "$cfg"
  fi
  chmod 600 "$cfg"
  runner_cfg_owner
}

# When the runner service runs as gitlab-runner (install/users.sh), it must own its config after root edits it.
runner_cfg_owner() {
  local cfg=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml
  if [[ -f /etc/systemd/system/gitlab-runner.service.d/10-service-user.conf ]] && id gitlab-runner >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    chown -R gitlab-runner:gitlab-runner /etc/gitlab-runner
    chmod 600 "$cfg"
  fi
}

# --- service user for the monitoring and logging stacks (created by install/users.sh) ---------------
MON_USER="${MON_USER:-monitoring}"

# Set MON_UID and MON_GID, or stop if the service user is missing.
mon_ids() {
  id "$MON_USER" >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "service user ${MON_USER} missing, run install/users.sh first"
  MON_UID=$(id -u "$MON_USER")
  MON_GID=$(id -g "$MON_USER")
}

# env_set FILE KEY VALUE: set KEY=VALUE in a root-only env file.
env_set() {
  local f="$1" k="$2" v="$3"
  touch "$f"
  chmod 600 "$f"
  if grep -q "^${k}=" "$f"; then
    sed -i "s|^${k}=.*|${k}=${v}|" "$f"
  else
    printf '%s=%s\n' "$k" "$v" >> "$f"
  fi
}

# stack_ids DIR [logging]: write the ids the compose file needs into DIR/.env.
stack_ids() {
  local dir="$1" docker_gid journal_gid
  mon_ids
  env_set "${dir}/.env" MON_UID "$MON_UID"
  env_set "${dir}/.env" MON_GID "$MON_GID"
  if [[ "${2:-}" == logging ]]; then
    docker_gid=$(getent group docker | cut -d: -f3)
    [[ -n "$docker_gid" ]] || die "docker group missing"
    env_set "${dir}/.env" DOCKER_GID "$docker_gid"
    journal_gid=$(getent group systemd-journal | cut -d: -f3)
    [[ -n "$journal_gid" ]] || die "systemd-journal group missing"
    # compose rejects equal group_add items, so the journal group is only added when it differs from docker's.
    if [[ "$journal_gid" != "$docker_gid" ]]; then
      printf 'services:\n  alloy:\n    group_add:\n      - "%s"\n' "$journal_gid" > "${dir}/docker-compose.override.yml"
    else
      rm -f "${dir}/docker-compose.override.yml"
    fi
  fi
}

# Config readable by the service user, writable by root only; .env (secrets) stays root-only.
own_stack_dir() {
  local dir="$1"
  chown -R "root:${MON_USER}" "$dir"
  chmod -R u=rwX,g=rX,o= "$dir"
  if [[ -f "${dir}/.env" ]]; then
    chown root:root "${dir}/.env"
    chmod 600 "${dir}/.env"
  fi
}

# Create the stack's containers and volumes without starting them, then hand the volumes to the service user.
chown_volumes() {
  local dir="$1" project v
  mon_ids
  project=$(cd "$dir" && docker compose config --format json | jq -r .name)
  (cd "$dir" && docker compose up --no-start --quiet-pull >/dev/null 2>&1) || (cd "$dir" && docker compose up --no-start >/dev/null)
  for v in $(docker volume ls -q --filter "label=com.docker.compose.project=${project}"); do
    docker run --rm --network none -v "${v}:/v" alpine:3.20 chown -R "${MON_UID}:${MON_GID}" /v
  done
}
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/install/lib.sh"

install -d "$R/install"
cat > "$R/install/monitoring.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Install Prometheus + node_exporter + Grafana (all bound to 127.0.0.1) with the runner dashboard.
set -Eeuo pipefail
# shellcheck source=install/lib.sh
source "$(dirname "$0")/lib.sh"

DIR=/opt/monitoring

if [[ "${1:-}" == "-h" || "${1:-}" == "--help" ]]; then
  echo "Usage: $0  installs the monitoring stack into $DIR"
  exit 0
fi
require_root
mon_ids
command -v docker >/dev/null || die "docker not installed"
docker compose version >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "docker compose plugin missing"
command -v jq >/dev/null || pkg_install jq
[[ -f "$DIR/docker-compose.yml" ]] && die "$DIR/docker-compose.yml exists, stack already installed"

selinux_label_dir "$DIR"
curl -fsS http://127.0.0.1:9252/metrics >/dev/null 2>&1 || log "WARN: runner metrics on 127.0.0.1:9252 not reachable"

cp -a "$REPO_ROOT/monitoring/docker-compose.yml" "$DIR/"
cp -a "$REPO_ROOT/monitoring/prometheus" "$REPO_ROOT/monitoring/grafana" "$DIR/"

if [[ ! -f "$DIR/.env" ]]; then
  pw=$(head -c 48 /dev/urandom | base64 | tr -dc 'A-Za-z0-9' | cut -c1-24)
  (umask 077; sed "s/^GF_SECURITY_ADMIN_PASSWORD=.*/GF_SECURITY_ADMIN_PASSWORD=${pw}/" "$REPO_ROOT/monitoring/env.example" | grep -v '^#' > "$DIR/.env")
  unset pw
fi
chmod 600 "$DIR/.env"
stack_ids "$DIR"
own_stack_dir "$DIR"
restorecon_if -R "$DIR"

prom_image=$(awk '/image: prom\/prometheus/ { print $2 }' "$DIR/docker-compose.yml")
jq empty "$DIR/grafana/dashboards/gitlab-runner.json"
docker compose -f "$DIR/docker-compose.yml" config -q
docker run --rm --user "${MON_UID}:${MON_GID}" --entrypoint promtool -v "$DIR/prometheus:/etc/prometheus:ro" "$prom_image" \
  check config /etc/prometheus/prometheus.yml

cd "$DIR"
docker compose pull -q
chown_volumes "$DIR"
docker compose up -d

for _ in $(seq 1 30); do
  curl -fsS http://127.0.0.1:9090/-/ready >/dev/null 2>&1 \
    && curl -fsS http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/health >/dev/null 2>&1 && break
  sleep 2
done
for _ in $(seq 1 15); do
  down=$(curl -fsS http://127.0.0.1:9090/api/v1/targets | jq '[.data.activeTargets[] | select(.health != "up")] | length')
  [[ "$down" == "0" ]] && break
  sleep 2
done

docker compose ps --format 'table {{.Service}}\t{{.State}}\t{{.Status}}'
curl -fsS http://127.0.0.1:9090/api/v1/targets | jq -r '.data.activeTargets[] | "\(.labels.job)\t\(.health)\t\(.lastError)"'
ss -ltn | awk 'NR==1 || /:(9090|9100|3000|9252) /'

exposed=$(ss -ltn | awk '/:(9090|9100|3000) / && $4 !~ /^127\.0\.0\.1:/' | wc -l)
[[ "$exposed" == "0" ]] || die "a monitoring port is not bound to 127.0.0.1"
log "OK: monitoring up. Grafana password: grep GF_SECURITY_ADMIN_PASSWORD $DIR/.env"
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/install/monitoring.sh"

install -d "$R/monitoring"
cat > "$R/monitoring/docker-compose.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
name: monitoring

services:
  prometheus:
    image: prom/prometheus:v3.4.0
    user: "${MON_UID:?run install/users.sh first}:${MON_GID:?run install/users.sh first}"
    restart: unless-stopped
    network_mode: host
    mem_limit: 512m
    command:
      - --config.file=/etc/prometheus/prometheus.yml
      - --storage.tsdb.path=/prometheus
      - --storage.tsdb.retention.time=15d
      - --storage.tsdb.retention.size=2GB
      - --web.listen-address=127.0.0.1:9090
      - --web.enable-lifecycle
    volumes:
      - ./prometheus/prometheus.yml:/etc/prometheus/prometheus.yml:ro
      - ./prometheus/alerts.yml:/etc/prometheus/alerts.yml:ro
      - prometheus-data:/prometheus

  node-exporter:
    image: prom/node-exporter:v1.9.1
    user: "${MON_UID:?run install/users.sh first}:${MON_GID:?run install/users.sh first}"
    restart: unless-stopped
    network_mode: host
    pid: host
    security_opt:
      - label=disable
    mem_limit: 64m
    command:
      - --path.rootfs=/host
      - --web.listen-address=127.0.0.1:9100
    volumes:
      - /:/host:ro,rslave

  grafana:
    image: grafana/grafana:12.0.0
    user: "${MON_UID:?run install/users.sh first}:${MON_GID:?run install/users.sh first}"
    restart: unless-stopped
    network_mode: host
    mem_limit: 256m
    env_file: .env
    depends_on: [prometheus]
    volumes:
      - ./grafana/provisioning:/etc/grafana/provisioning:ro
      - ./grafana/dashboards:/var/lib/grafana/dashboards:ro
      - grafana-data:/var/lib/grafana

volumes:
  prometheus-data:
  grafana-data:
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/monitoring/docker-compose.yml"

install -d "$R/monitoring"
cat > "$R/monitoring/env.example" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
# Copied to /opt/monitoring/.env by install/monitoring.sh; the password is generated there.
GF_SECURITY_ADMIN_USER=admin
GF_SECURITY_ADMIN_PASSWORD=generated-on-install
GF_SERVER_HTTP_ADDR=127.0.0.1
GF_SERVER_HTTP_PORT=3000
GF_USERS_ALLOW_SIGN_UP=false
GF_AUTH_ANONYMOUS_ENABLED=false
GF_ANALYTICS_REPORTING_ENABLED=false
GF_ANALYTICS_CHECK_FOR_UPDATES=false
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/monitoring/env.example"

install -d "$R/monitoring/prometheus"
cat > "$R/monitoring/prometheus/alerts.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
groups:
  - name: runner-host
    rules:
      - alert: GitLabRunnerDown
        expr: up{job="gitlab-runner"} == 0
        for: 2m
        labels:
          severity: critical
        annotations:
          summary: GitLab Runner metrics endpoint is down
      - alert: DiskSpaceHigh
        expr: (1 - node_filesystem_avail_bytes{mountpoint="/",fstype!="rootfs"} / node_filesystem_size_bytes{mountpoint="/",fstype!="rootfs"}) * 100 > 80
        for: 10m
        labels:
          severity: warning
        annotations:
          summary: Root filesystem usage above 80%
      - alert: MemoryHigh
        expr: (1 - node_memory_MemAvailable_bytes / node_memory_MemTotal_bytes) * 100 > 90
        for: 10m
        labels:
          severity: warning
        annotations:
          summary: Memory usage above 90%
      - alert: RunnerJobFailures
        expr: sum(increase(gitlab_runner_failed_jobs_total[30m])) > 3
        for: 0m
        labels:
          severity: warning
        annotations:
          summary: More than 3 runner job failures in 30 minutes
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/monitoring/prometheus/alerts.yml"

install -d "$R/monitoring/prometheus"
cat > "$R/monitoring/prometheus/prometheus.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
global:
  scrape_interval: 15s
  evaluation_interval: 15s

rule_files:
  - /etc/prometheus/alerts.yml

scrape_configs:
  - job_name: prometheus
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:9090"]
  - job_name: node
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:9100"]
  - job_name: gitlab-runner
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:9252"]
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/monitoring/prometheus/prometheus.yml"

install -d "$R/monitoring/grafana/dashboards"
cat > "$R/monitoring/grafana/dashboards/gitlab-runner.json" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
{
  "uid": "gitlab-runner-host",
  "title": "GitLab Runner Host",
  "schemaVersion": 39,
  "version": 1,
  "refresh": "30s",
  "time": { "from": "now-6h", "to": "now" },
  "tags": ["gitlab", "runner"],
  "panels": [
    {
      "id": 1, "type": "stat", "title": "Runner up",
      "gridPos": { "h": 4, "w": 6, "x": 0, "y": 0 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "fieldConfig": { "defaults": { "mappings": [ { "type": "value", "options": { "0": { "text": "DOWN", "color": "red" }, "1": { "text": "UP", "color": "green" } } } ], "color": { "mode": "thresholds" }, "thresholds": { "mode": "absolute", "steps": [ { "color": "red", "value": null }, { "color": "green", "value": 1 } ] } } },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "up{job=\"gitlab-runner\"}" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 2, "type": "stat", "title": "Running jobs",
      "gridPos": { "h": 4, "w": 6, "x": 6, "y": 0 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "sum(gitlab_runner_jobs) or vector(0)" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 3, "type": "stat", "title": "Concurrent limit",
      "gridPos": { "h": 4, "w": 6, "x": 12, "y": 0 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "gitlab_runner_concurrent" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 4, "type": "gauge", "title": "Disk used",
      "gridPos": { "h": 4, "w": 6, "x": 18, "y": 0 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "fieldConfig": { "defaults": { "unit": "percent", "min": 0, "max": 100, "thresholds": { "mode": "absolute", "steps": [ { "color": "green", "value": null }, { "color": "orange", "value": 70 }, { "color": "red", "value": 85 } ] } } },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "(1 - node_filesystem_avail_bytes{mountpoint=\"/\",fstype!=\"rootfs\"} / node_filesystem_size_bytes{mountpoint=\"/\",fstype!=\"rootfs\"}) * 100" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 5, "type": "timeseries", "title": "Jobs by stage",
      "gridPos": { "h": 8, "w": 12, "x": 0, "y": 4 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "sum by (executor_stage) (gitlab_runner_jobs)", "legendFormat": "{{executor_stage}}" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 6, "type": "timeseries", "title": "Job duration p95",
      "gridPos": { "h": 8, "w": 12, "x": 12, "y": 4 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "fieldConfig": { "defaults": { "unit": "s" } },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "histogram_quantile(0.95, sum by (le) (rate(gitlab_runner_job_duration_seconds_bucket[1h])))", "legendFormat": "p95" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 7, "type": "timeseries", "title": "CPU usage",
      "gridPos": { "h": 8, "w": 8, "x": 0, "y": 12 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "fieldConfig": { "defaults": { "unit": "percent", "min": 0, "max": 100 } },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "100 - avg(rate(node_cpu_seconds_total{mode=\"idle\"}[5m])) * 100", "legendFormat": "cpu" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 8, "type": "timeseries", "title": "Memory usage",
      "gridPos": { "h": 8, "w": 8, "x": 8, "y": 12 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "fieldConfig": { "defaults": { "unit": "percent", "min": 0, "max": 100 } },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "(1 - node_memory_MemAvailable_bytes / node_memory_MemTotal_bytes) * 100", "legendFormat": "memory" } ]
    },
    {
      "id": 9, "type": "timeseries", "title": "Failed jobs (1h)",
      "gridPos": { "h": 8, "w": 8, "x": 16, "y": 12 },
      "datasource": { "type": "prometheus", "uid": "prometheus" },
      "targets": [ { "refId": "A", "expr": "sum by (failure_reason) (increase(gitlab_runner_failed_jobs_total[1h]))", "legendFormat": "{{failure_reason}}" } ]
    }
  ]
}
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/monitoring/grafana/dashboards/gitlab-runner.json"

install -d "$R/monitoring/grafana/provisioning/dashboards"
cat > "$R/monitoring/grafana/provisioning/dashboards/default.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
apiVersion: 1
providers:
  - name: default
    folder: GitLab Runner
    type: file
    disableDeletion: true
    allowUiUpdates: false
    options:
      path: /var/lib/grafana/dashboards
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/monitoring/grafana/provisioning/dashboards/default.yml"

install -d "$R/monitoring/grafana/provisioning/datasources"
cat > "$R/monitoring/grafana/provisioning/datasources/prometheus.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
apiVersion: 1
datasources:
  - name: Prometheus
    uid: prometheus
    type: prometheus
    access: proxy
    url: http://127.0.0.1:9090
    isDefault: true
    editable: false
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/monitoring/grafana/provisioning/datasources/prometheus.yml"

echo "files written to $R: 9"
bash "$R/install/monitoring.sh" </dev/null
INSTALL
````

<a id="logging"></a>
## Logging: Loki + Grafana Alloy

Container logs (not CI jobs) and the journal, 7 days. Run after monitoring so Grafana gets the Loki datasource.

````bash
sudo bash <<'INSTALL'
set -Eeuo pipefail
R=/root/server-init-config

install -d "$R/install"
cat > "$R/install/lib.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Shared helpers for install/*.sh. Sourced, not executed.
# shellcheck shell=bash

REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
export REPO_ROOT

log() { echo "[$(date +%H:%M:%S)] $*"; }
die() { echo "ERROR: $*" >&2; exit 1; }

require_root() { [[ $EUID -eq 0 ]] || die "run as root"; }

selinux_on() { command -v selinuxenabled >/dev/null 2>&1 && selinuxenabled; }

pkg_install() {
  if command -v dnf >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    dnf -y -q install "$@"
  elif command -v apt-get >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    apt-get update -q && apt-get install -y -q "$@"
  else
    die "no supported package manager"
  fi
}

# Label a directory so containers may read files in it (no-op without SELinux).
selinux_label_dir() {
  local dir="$1"
  install -d -m 0755 "$dir"
  selinux_on || return 0
  command -v semanage >/dev/null 2>&1 || pkg_install policycoreutils-python-utils
  # Read the whole list first: grep -q exits at the first match and would SIGPIPE the producer under pipefail.
  if ! grep -q "^${dir}(/\.\*)?" <<<"$(semanage fcontext -l)"; then
    semanage fcontext -a -t container_file_t "${dir}(/.*)?"
  fi
  restorecon -R "$dir"
}

restorecon_if() { if command -v restorecon >/dev/null 2>&1; then restorecon "$@" || true; fi; }

# RHEL-family sudo resets PATH to secure_path, which can leave out /usr/local/bin (so `sudo health` fails).
# Adds a validated drop-in unless some sudoers file already sets a secure_path that includes it.
ensure_secure_path() {
  local f=/etc/sudoers.d/50-secure-path tmp
  command -v visudo >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 0
  if grep -rhsE '^[[:space:]]*Defaults[[:space:]]+secure_path[[:space:]]*=.*/usr/local/bin' /etc/sudoers /etc/sudoers.d 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then
    return 0
  fi
  if ! grep -rhsE '^[[:space:]]*Defaults[[:space:]]+secure_path' /etc/sudoers /etc/sudoers.d 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then
    return 0   # no secure_path at all (Debian family keeps it in sudoers; others use the caller PATH)
  fi
  tmp=$(mktemp)
  printf 'Defaults secure_path="/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin"\n' > "$tmp"
  if visudo -cqf "$tmp"; then
    install -d -m 0750 /etc/sudoers.d
    install -m 0440 -o root -g root "$tmp" "$f"
    log "sudo secure_path now includes /usr/local/bin (${f})"
  else
    echo "WARN: generated sudoers drop-in is invalid, secure_path left as it was" >&2
  fi
  rm -f "$tmp"
}

ci_jobs_running() { docker ps -q --filter "label=com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed=true" 2>/dev/null | wc -l; }

require_runner_idle() {
  local n
  n=$(ci_jobs_running)
  (( n == 0 )) || die "${n} CI job container(s) running, retry when the runner is idle"
}

backup() { cp -a "$1" "${1}.bak.$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)"; }

# Prometheus scrape jobs for the logging stack, shared by install/logging.sh and install/update.sh.
prom_jobs_logging() {
  cat <<'YAML'
  - job_name: loki
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:3100"]
  - job_name: alloy
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:12345"]
YAML
}

# Merge KEY=VALUE entries into the single [[runners]] environment line of config.toml.
runner_env_merge() {
  local cfg=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml existing list key e line
  [[ $(grep -c '^\[\[runners\]\]' "$cfg") -eq 1 ]] || die "expected exactly one [[runners]] block in $cfg"
  existing=$(grep -E '^  environment = \[' "$cfg" | sed -E 's/^  environment = \[(.*)\]$/\1/' || true)
  list="$existing"
  for e in "$@"; do
    key="${e%%=*}"
    if grep -q "\"${key}=" <<<"$list"; then
      list=$(sed -E "s|\"${key}=[^\"]*\"|\"${e}\"|" <<<"$list")
    else
      list="${list:+${list}, }\"${e}\""
    fi
  done
  line="  environment = [${list}]"
  if grep -qE '^  environment = \[' "$cfg"; then
    sed -i -E "s|^  environment = \[.*\]$|${line}|" "$cfg"
  else
    sed -i -E "0,/^  executor = .*/s||${line}\n&|" "$cfg"
  fi
  chmod 600 "$cfg"
  runner_cfg_owner
}

# When the runner service runs as gitlab-runner (install/users.sh), it must own its config after root edits it.
runner_cfg_owner() {
  local cfg=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml
  if [[ -f /etc/systemd/system/gitlab-runner.service.d/10-service-user.conf ]] && id gitlab-runner >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    chown -R gitlab-runner:gitlab-runner /etc/gitlab-runner
    chmod 600 "$cfg"
  fi
}

# --- service user for the monitoring and logging stacks (created by install/users.sh) ---------------
MON_USER="${MON_USER:-monitoring}"

# Set MON_UID and MON_GID, or stop if the service user is missing.
mon_ids() {
  id "$MON_USER" >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "service user ${MON_USER} missing, run install/users.sh first"
  MON_UID=$(id -u "$MON_USER")
  MON_GID=$(id -g "$MON_USER")
}

# env_set FILE KEY VALUE: set KEY=VALUE in a root-only env file.
env_set() {
  local f="$1" k="$2" v="$3"
  touch "$f"
  chmod 600 "$f"
  if grep -q "^${k}=" "$f"; then
    sed -i "s|^${k}=.*|${k}=${v}|" "$f"
  else
    printf '%s=%s\n' "$k" "$v" >> "$f"
  fi
}

# stack_ids DIR [logging]: write the ids the compose file needs into DIR/.env.
stack_ids() {
  local dir="$1" docker_gid journal_gid
  mon_ids
  env_set "${dir}/.env" MON_UID "$MON_UID"
  env_set "${dir}/.env" MON_GID "$MON_GID"
  if [[ "${2:-}" == logging ]]; then
    docker_gid=$(getent group docker | cut -d: -f3)
    [[ -n "$docker_gid" ]] || die "docker group missing"
    env_set "${dir}/.env" DOCKER_GID "$docker_gid"
    journal_gid=$(getent group systemd-journal | cut -d: -f3)
    [[ -n "$journal_gid" ]] || die "systemd-journal group missing"
    # compose rejects equal group_add items, so the journal group is only added when it differs from docker's.
    if [[ "$journal_gid" != "$docker_gid" ]]; then
      printf 'services:\n  alloy:\n    group_add:\n      - "%s"\n' "$journal_gid" > "${dir}/docker-compose.override.yml"
    else
      rm -f "${dir}/docker-compose.override.yml"
    fi
  fi
}

# Config readable by the service user, writable by root only; .env (secrets) stays root-only.
own_stack_dir() {
  local dir="$1"
  chown -R "root:${MON_USER}" "$dir"
  chmod -R u=rwX,g=rX,o= "$dir"
  if [[ -f "${dir}/.env" ]]; then
    chown root:root "${dir}/.env"
    chmod 600 "${dir}/.env"
  fi
}

# Create the stack's containers and volumes without starting them, then hand the volumes to the service user.
chown_volumes() {
  local dir="$1" project v
  mon_ids
  project=$(cd "$dir" && docker compose config --format json | jq -r .name)
  (cd "$dir" && docker compose up --no-start --quiet-pull >/dev/null 2>&1) || (cd "$dir" && docker compose up --no-start >/dev/null)
  for v in $(docker volume ls -q --filter "label=com.docker.compose.project=${project}"); do
    docker run --rm --network none -v "${v}:/v" alpine:3.20 chown -R "${MON_UID}:${MON_GID}" /v
  done
}
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/install/lib.sh"

install -d "$R/install"
cat > "$R/install/logging.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Log collection: Loki (storage, 7 days) + Grafana Alloy (container logs and the systemd journal).
# CI job containers are not collected, their output is the job log in GitLab.
# Alloy reads the Docker socket, which is root-equivalent on the host. Everything listens on 127.0.0.1.
set -Eeuo pipefail
# shellcheck source=install/lib.sh
source "$(dirname "$0")/lib.sh"

DIR=/opt/logging
MON=/opt/monitoring
PROM_CFG="${MON}/prometheus/prometheus.yml"
DATASOURCE="${MON}/grafana/provisioning/datasources/loki.yml"

if [[ "${1:-}" == "-h" || "${1:-}" == "--help" ]]; then
  echo "Usage: $0  installs Loki + Grafana Alloy into $DIR and adds Loki to Grafana if monitoring is installed"
  exit 0
fi
require_root
mon_ids
command -v docker >/dev/null || die "docker not installed"
docker compose version >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "docker compose plugin missing"
command -v jq >/dev/null || pkg_install jq
if [[ -f "${DIR}/docker-compose.yml" ]]; then
  log "${DIR} exists: re-applying the repo files and finishing the setup (safe after a failed run)"
elif ss -ltn | awk '$4 ~ /:(3100|9096|12345)$/' | grep -q .; then
  die "port 3100, 9096 or 12345 already in use"
fi
[[ -f /etc/machine-id ]] || die "/etc/machine-id missing, the journal cannot be read"

if [[ ! -d /var/log/journal ]]; then
  log "enabling persistent journal in /var/log/journal"
  install -d -m 2755 -g systemd-journal /var/log/journal
  systemd-tmpfiles --create --prefix /var/log/journal
  systemctl restart systemd-journald
fi

selinux_label_dir "$DIR"
cp -a "${REPO_ROOT}/logging/docker-compose.yml" "${REPO_ROOT}/logging/loki.yaml" "${REPO_ROOT}/logging/config.alloy" "${DIR}/"
stack_ids "$DIR" logging
own_stack_dir "$DIR"
restorecon_if -R "$DIR"

loki_image=$(awk '/image: grafana\/loki/ { print $2 }' "${DIR}/docker-compose.yml")
alloy_image=$(awk '/image: grafana\/alloy/ { print $2 }' "${DIR}/docker-compose.yml")

cd "$DIR"
docker compose config -q
docker compose pull -q
docker run --rm --user "${MON_UID}:${MON_GID}" -v "${DIR}/loki.yaml:/etc/loki/config.yaml:ro" "$loki_image" \
  -config.file=/etc/loki/config.yaml -verify-config || die "Loki rejected loki.yaml"
docker run --rm --user "${MON_UID}:${MON_GID}" --entrypoint /bin/alloy -v "${DIR}/config.alloy:/config/config.alloy:ro" "$alloy_image" \
  fmt /config/config.alloy >/dev/null || die "Alloy rejected config.alloy"
chown_volumes "$DIR"
docker compose up -d --force-recreate

ready=0
for _ in $(seq 1 45); do
  if curl -fsS -m 3 http://127.0.0.1:3100/ready >/dev/null 2>&1 && curl -fsS -m 3 http://127.0.0.1:12345/-/ready >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    ready=1
    break
  fi
  sleep 2
done
if (( ! ready )); then
  docker compose logs --tail 40
  die "Loki or Alloy did not become ready"
fi

if [[ -d "${MON}/grafana/provisioning/datasources" ]]; then
  cp "${REPO_ROOT}/logging/grafana-datasource.yml" "$DATASOURCE"
  chown "root:${MON_USER}" "$DATASOURCE"
  chmod 640 "$DATASOURCE"
  restorecon_if "$DATASOURCE"
  (cd "$MON" && docker compose restart grafana >/dev/null)
  log "Loki added to Grafana as a datasource"
fi
if [[ -f "$PROM_CFG" ]] && ! grep -q 'job_name: loki' "$PROM_CFG"; then
  backup "$PROM_CFG"
  prom_jobs_logging >> "$PROM_CFG"
  curl -fsS -X POST http://127.0.0.1:9090/-/reload || log "WARN: prometheus reload failed"
fi

marker="logging-install-check-$(date +%s)"
logger -t logging-install -- "$marker"
found=0
for _ in $(seq 1 30); do
  n=$(curl -fsS -G -m 5 http://127.0.0.1:3100/loki/api/v1/query_range \
        --data-urlencode "query={source=\"journal\"} |= \"${marker}\"" --data-urlencode "since=10m" 2>/dev/null \
      | jq '.data.result | length' 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
  if [[ "${n:-0}" != "0" ]]; then
    found=1
    break
  fi
  sleep 2
done
if (( found )); then
  log "journal -> Alloy -> Loki works"
else
  log "WARN: test line not in Loki yet, check: docker compose -f ${DIR}/docker-compose.yml logs alloy"
fi

docker compose ps --format 'table {{.Service}}\t{{.State}}\t{{.Status}}'
exposed=$(ss -ltn | awk '/:(3100|9096|12345) / && $4 !~ /^127\.0\.0\.1:/' | wc -l)
[[ "$exposed" == "0" ]] || die "a logging port is not bound to 127.0.0.1"
log "OK: logs in Grafana > Explore > Loki, e.g. {unit=\"gitlab-runner.service\"} or {compose_project=\"registry-cache\"}"
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/install/logging.sh"

install -d "$R/logging"
cat > "$R/logging/config.alloy" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
// Container logs and the systemd journal to the local Loki.
// CI job containers are skipped: their output is already the job log in GitLab.

logging {
  level = "warn"
}

loki.write "local" {
  endpoint {
    url = "http://127.0.0.1:3100/loki/api/v1/push"
  }
}

discovery.docker "containers" {
  host             = "unix:///var/run/docker.sock"
  refresh_interval = "15s"
}

discovery.relabel "containers" {
  targets = []

  rule {
    source_labels = ["__meta_docker_container_label_com_gitlab_gitlab_runner_managed"]
    regex         = "true"
    action        = "drop"
  }

  rule {
    source_labels = ["__meta_docker_container_name"]
    regex         = "/(.*)"
    target_label  = "container"
  }

  rule {
    source_labels = ["__meta_docker_container_label_com_docker_compose_project"]
    target_label  = "compose_project"
  }

  rule {
    source_labels = ["__meta_docker_container_label_com_docker_compose_service"]
    target_label  = "compose_service"
  }
}

loki.source.docker "containers" {
  host          = "unix:///var/run/docker.sock"
  targets       = discovery.docker.containers.targets
  labels        = {source = "docker"}
  relabel_rules = discovery.relabel.containers.rules
  forward_to    = [loki.write.local.receiver]
}

loki.relabel "journal" {
  forward_to = []

  rule {
    source_labels = ["__journal__systemd_unit"]
    target_label  = "unit"
  }

  rule {
    source_labels = ["__journal_priority_keyword"]
    target_label  = "level"
  }

  rule {
    source_labels = ["__journal_syslog_identifier"]
    target_label  = "identifier"
  }
}

loki.source.journal "system" {
  path          = "/var/log/journal"
  max_age       = "12h"
  labels        = {source = "journal"}
  relabel_rules = loki.relabel.journal.rules
  forward_to    = [loki.write.local.receiver]
}
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/logging/config.alloy"

install -d "$R/logging"
cat > "$R/logging/docker-compose.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
name: logging

services:
  loki:
    image: grafana/loki:3.7.7
    user: "${MON_UID:?run install/users.sh first}:${MON_GID:?run install/users.sh first}"
    restart: unless-stopped
    network_mode: host
    mem_limit: 512m
    command: ["-config.file=/etc/loki/config.yaml"]
    volumes:
      - ./loki.yaml:/etc/loki/config.yaml:ro
      - loki-data:/loki

  # Runs as the monitoring service user. Docker socket and journal access come from group_add
  # (docker and systemd-journal); note that reading the Docker socket is root-equivalent on the host.
  # The journal group is added by docker-compose.override.yml, which stack_ids (install/lib.sh) writes
  # only when its gid differs from the docker gid: compose rejects a group_add list with equal items.
  alloy:
    image: grafana/alloy:v1.19.2
    restart: unless-stopped
    network_mode: host
    mem_limit: 256m
    user: "${MON_UID:?run install/users.sh first}:${MON_GID:?run install/users.sh first}"
    group_add:
      - "${DOCKER_GID:?run install/logging.sh}"
    read_only: true
    cap_drop: [ALL]
    security_opt:
      - label=disable
      - no-new-privileges:true
    tmpfs:
      - /tmp
    command:
      - run
      - --server.http.listen-addr=127.0.0.1:12345
      - --storage.path=/data
      - --disable-reporting
      - /config/config.alloy
    volumes:
      - ./config.alloy:/config/config.alloy:ro
      - alloy-data:/data
      - /var/run/docker.sock:/var/run/docker.sock:ro
      - /var/log/journal:/var/log/journal:ro
      - /etc/machine-id:/etc/machine-id:ro
    depends_on: [loki]

volumes:
  loki-data:
  alloy-data:
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/logging/docker-compose.yml"

install -d "$R/logging"
cat > "$R/logging/grafana-datasource.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
apiVersion: 1
datasources:
  - name: Loki
    uid: loki
    type: loki
    access: proxy
    url: http://127.0.0.1:3100
    editable: false
    jsonData:
      maxLines: 1000
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/logging/grafana-datasource.yml"

install -d "$R/logging"
cat > "$R/logging/loki.yaml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
auth_enabled: false

server:
  http_listen_address: 127.0.0.1
  http_listen_port: 3100
  grpc_listen_address: 127.0.0.1
  grpc_listen_port: 9096
  log_level: warn

common:
  instance_addr: 127.0.0.1
  path_prefix: /loki
  replication_factor: 1
  storage:
    filesystem:
      chunks_directory: /loki/chunks
      rules_directory: /loki/rules
  ring:
    kvstore:
      store: inmemory

schema_config:
  configs:
    - from: 2024-04-01
      store: tsdb
      object_store: filesystem
      schema: v13
      index:
        prefix: index_
        period: 24h

limits_config:
  retention_period: 168h
  ingestion_rate_mb: 4
  ingestion_burst_size_mb: 8
  max_query_series: 5000
  allow_structured_metadata: true
  volume_enabled: true

compactor:
  working_directory: /loki/compactor
  compaction_interval: 10m
  retention_enabled: true
  retention_delete_delay: 2h
  delete_request_store: filesystem

analytics:
  reporting_enabled: false
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/logging/loki.yaml"

echo "files written to $R: 6"
bash "$R/install/logging.sh" </dev/null
INSTALL
````

<a id="npm-cache"></a>
## npm cache (Verdaccio)

Needs a registered runner. Points `npm` and `yarn` in jobs at the cache. Run it while no jobs are running. Verdaccio binds to the docker0 IP: if it changes (`bip` in daemon.json), rerun this block or `install/update.sh`.

````bash
sudo bash <<'INSTALL'
set -Eeuo pipefail
R=/root/server-init-config

install -d "$R/install"
cat > "$R/install/lib.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Shared helpers for install/*.sh. Sourced, not executed.
# shellcheck shell=bash

REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
export REPO_ROOT

log() { echo "[$(date +%H:%M:%S)] $*"; }
die() { echo "ERROR: $*" >&2; exit 1; }

require_root() { [[ $EUID -eq 0 ]] || die "run as root"; }

selinux_on() { command -v selinuxenabled >/dev/null 2>&1 && selinuxenabled; }

pkg_install() {
  if command -v dnf >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    dnf -y -q install "$@"
  elif command -v apt-get >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    apt-get update -q && apt-get install -y -q "$@"
  else
    die "no supported package manager"
  fi
}

# Label a directory so containers may read files in it (no-op without SELinux).
selinux_label_dir() {
  local dir="$1"
  install -d -m 0755 "$dir"
  selinux_on || return 0
  command -v semanage >/dev/null 2>&1 || pkg_install policycoreutils-python-utils
  # Read the whole list first: grep -q exits at the first match and would SIGPIPE the producer under pipefail.
  if ! grep -q "^${dir}(/\.\*)?" <<<"$(semanage fcontext -l)"; then
    semanage fcontext -a -t container_file_t "${dir}(/.*)?"
  fi
  restorecon -R "$dir"
}

restorecon_if() { if command -v restorecon >/dev/null 2>&1; then restorecon "$@" || true; fi; }

# RHEL-family sudo resets PATH to secure_path, which can leave out /usr/local/bin (so `sudo health` fails).
# Adds a validated drop-in unless some sudoers file already sets a secure_path that includes it.
ensure_secure_path() {
  local f=/etc/sudoers.d/50-secure-path tmp
  command -v visudo >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 0
  if grep -rhsE '^[[:space:]]*Defaults[[:space:]]+secure_path[[:space:]]*=.*/usr/local/bin' /etc/sudoers /etc/sudoers.d 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then
    return 0
  fi
  if ! grep -rhsE '^[[:space:]]*Defaults[[:space:]]+secure_path' /etc/sudoers /etc/sudoers.d 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then
    return 0   # no secure_path at all (Debian family keeps it in sudoers; others use the caller PATH)
  fi
  tmp=$(mktemp)
  printf 'Defaults secure_path="/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin"\n' > "$tmp"
  if visudo -cqf "$tmp"; then
    install -d -m 0750 /etc/sudoers.d
    install -m 0440 -o root -g root "$tmp" "$f"
    log "sudo secure_path now includes /usr/local/bin (${f})"
  else
    echo "WARN: generated sudoers drop-in is invalid, secure_path left as it was" >&2
  fi
  rm -f "$tmp"
}

ci_jobs_running() { docker ps -q --filter "label=com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed=true" 2>/dev/null | wc -l; }

require_runner_idle() {
  local n
  n=$(ci_jobs_running)
  (( n == 0 )) || die "${n} CI job container(s) running, retry when the runner is idle"
}

backup() { cp -a "$1" "${1}.bak.$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)"; }

# Prometheus scrape jobs for the logging stack, shared by install/logging.sh and install/update.sh.
prom_jobs_logging() {
  cat <<'YAML'
  - job_name: loki
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:3100"]
  - job_name: alloy
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:12345"]
YAML
}

# Merge KEY=VALUE entries into the single [[runners]] environment line of config.toml.
runner_env_merge() {
  local cfg=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml existing list key e line
  [[ $(grep -c '^\[\[runners\]\]' "$cfg") -eq 1 ]] || die "expected exactly one [[runners]] block in $cfg"
  existing=$(grep -E '^  environment = \[' "$cfg" | sed -E 's/^  environment = \[(.*)\]$/\1/' || true)
  list="$existing"
  for e in "$@"; do
    key="${e%%=*}"
    if grep -q "\"${key}=" <<<"$list"; then
      list=$(sed -E "s|\"${key}=[^\"]*\"|\"${e}\"|" <<<"$list")
    else
      list="${list:+${list}, }\"${e}\""
    fi
  done
  line="  environment = [${list}]"
  if grep -qE '^  environment = \[' "$cfg"; then
    sed -i -E "s|^  environment = \[.*\]$|${line}|" "$cfg"
  else
    sed -i -E "0,/^  executor = .*/s||${line}\n&|" "$cfg"
  fi
  chmod 600 "$cfg"
  runner_cfg_owner
}

# When the runner service runs as gitlab-runner (install/users.sh), it must own its config after root edits it.
runner_cfg_owner() {
  local cfg=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml
  if [[ -f /etc/systemd/system/gitlab-runner.service.d/10-service-user.conf ]] && id gitlab-runner >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    chown -R gitlab-runner:gitlab-runner /etc/gitlab-runner
    chmod 600 "$cfg"
  fi
}

# --- service user for the monitoring and logging stacks (created by install/users.sh) ---------------
MON_USER="${MON_USER:-monitoring}"

# Set MON_UID and MON_GID, or stop if the service user is missing.
mon_ids() {
  id "$MON_USER" >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "service user ${MON_USER} missing, run install/users.sh first"
  MON_UID=$(id -u "$MON_USER")
  MON_GID=$(id -g "$MON_USER")
}

# env_set FILE KEY VALUE: set KEY=VALUE in a root-only env file.
env_set() {
  local f="$1" k="$2" v="$3"
  touch "$f"
  chmod 600 "$f"
  if grep -q "^${k}=" "$f"; then
    sed -i "s|^${k}=.*|${k}=${v}|" "$f"
  else
    printf '%s=%s\n' "$k" "$v" >> "$f"
  fi
}

# stack_ids DIR [logging]: write the ids the compose file needs into DIR/.env.
stack_ids() {
  local dir="$1" docker_gid journal_gid
  mon_ids
  env_set "${dir}/.env" MON_UID "$MON_UID"
  env_set "${dir}/.env" MON_GID "$MON_GID"
  if [[ "${2:-}" == logging ]]; then
    docker_gid=$(getent group docker | cut -d: -f3)
    [[ -n "$docker_gid" ]] || die "docker group missing"
    env_set "${dir}/.env" DOCKER_GID "$docker_gid"
    journal_gid=$(getent group systemd-journal | cut -d: -f3)
    [[ -n "$journal_gid" ]] || die "systemd-journal group missing"
    # compose rejects equal group_add items, so the journal group is only added when it differs from docker's.
    if [[ "$journal_gid" != "$docker_gid" ]]; then
      printf 'services:\n  alloy:\n    group_add:\n      - "%s"\n' "$journal_gid" > "${dir}/docker-compose.override.yml"
    else
      rm -f "${dir}/docker-compose.override.yml"
    fi
  fi
}

# Config readable by the service user, writable by root only; .env (secrets) stays root-only.
own_stack_dir() {
  local dir="$1"
  chown -R "root:${MON_USER}" "$dir"
  chmod -R u=rwX,g=rX,o= "$dir"
  if [[ -f "${dir}/.env" ]]; then
    chown root:root "${dir}/.env"
    chmod 600 "${dir}/.env"
  fi
}

# Create the stack's containers and volumes without starting them, then hand the volumes to the service user.
chown_volumes() {
  local dir="$1" project v
  mon_ids
  project=$(cd "$dir" && docker compose config --format json | jq -r .name)
  (cd "$dir" && docker compose up --no-start --quiet-pull >/dev/null 2>&1) || (cd "$dir" && docker compose up --no-start >/dev/null)
  for v in $(docker volume ls -q --filter "label=com.docker.compose.project=${project}"); do
    docker run --rm --network none -v "${v}:/v" alpine:3.20 chown -R "${MON_UID}:${MON_GID}" /v
  done
}
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/install/lib.sh"

install -d "$R/install"
cat > "$R/install/npm-cache.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Verdaccio npm pull-through cache on the docker0 bridge IP, exposed to CI jobs via runner env.
# If the cache is down, npm ci in jobs fails (npm has no automatic fallback).
set -Eeuo pipefail
# shellcheck source=install/lib.sh
source "$(dirname "$0")/lib.sh"

DIR=/opt/npm-cache
CFG=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml

if [[ "${1:-}" == "-h" || "${1:-}" == "--help" ]]; then
  echo "Usage: $0  installs Verdaccio into $DIR and points runner jobs at it"
  exit 0
fi
require_root
if ! { [[ -f "$CFG" ]] && grep -q '^\[\[runners\]\]' "$CFG"; }; then die "runner not registered yet, run install/register-runner.sh first"; fi

BRIDGE_IP=$(ip -4 -o addr show docker0 2>/dev/null | awk '{ print $4 }' | cut -d/ -f1)
[[ -n "$BRIDGE_IP" ]] || die "docker0 bridge not found"
REG="http://${BRIDGE_IP}:4873/"
require_runner_idle

selinux_label_dir "$DIR"
sed "s/172\.17\.0\.1:4873/${BRIDGE_IP}:4873/" "$REPO_ROOT/npm-cache/config.yaml" > "$DIR/config.yaml"
sed "s/172\.17\.0\.1:4873/${BRIDGE_IP}:4873/" "$REPO_ROOT/npm-cache/docker-compose.yml" > "$DIR/docker-compose.yml"
restorecon_if -R "$DIR"

cd "$DIR"
docker compose config -q
docker compose pull -q
docker compose up -d
for _ in $(seq 1 20); do curl -fsS "${REG}-/ping" >/dev/null 2>&1 && break; sleep 2; done
curl -fsS "${REG}-/ping" >/dev/null || { docker compose logs --tail 30; die "verdaccio did not start"; }

exposed=$(ss -ltn | awk -v want="${BRIDGE_IP}:4873" '/:4873 / && $4 != want' | wc -l)
[[ "$exposed" == "0" ]] || { ss -ltn | awk '/:4873 /'; die "port 4873 is not bound to ${BRIDGE_IP} only"; }

backup "$CFG"
runner_env_merge "npm_config_registry=${REG}" "YARN_NPM_REGISTRY_SERVER=${REG}" "YARN_UNSAFE_HTTP_WHITELIST=${BRIDGE_IP}"
systemctl restart gitlab-runner
sleep 3

docker run --rm -e npm_config_registry="$REG" node:22-alpine sh -c \
  'cd /tmp && npm init -y >/dev/null && npm install --no-audit --no-fund lodash >/dev/null && echo "npm install via cache OK"' \
  || die "npm install through verdaccio failed"

grep -n 'environment =' "$CFG"
ss -ltn | awk 'NR==1 || /:4873 /'
gitlab-runner verify 2>&1 | grep -E 'is valid|is not valid' || true
log "OK: npm cache at ${REG}"
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/install/npm-cache.sh"

install -d "$R/npm-cache"
cat > "$R/npm-cache/config.yaml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
storage: /verdaccio/storage/data
plugins: /verdaccio/plugins

listen:
  - 172.17.0.1:4873

auth:
  htpasswd:
    file: /verdaccio/storage/htpasswd
    max_users: -1

uplinks:
  npmjs:
    url: https://registry.npmjs.org/
    timeout: 60s
    maxage: 30m
    cache: true

packages:
  '@*/*':
    access: $all
    publish: $authenticated
    proxy: npmjs
  '**':
    access: $all
    publish: $authenticated
    proxy: npmjs

server:
  keepAliveTimeout: 60

web:
  enable: false

log:
  type: stdout
  format: pretty
  level: warn
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/npm-cache/config.yaml"

install -d "$R/npm-cache"
cat > "$R/npm-cache/docker-compose.yml" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
name: npm-cache

services:
  verdaccio:
    image: verdaccio/verdaccio:6.1.2
    restart: unless-stopped
    network_mode: host
    mem_limit: 256m
    # The image's default command passes --listen 0.0.0.0:4873, which overrides "listen:" in config.yaml
    # and exposes the cache (access: $all) on every interface. install/npm-cache.sh and update.sh
    # replace 172.17.0.1 below with the docker0 IP, as they do in config.yaml.
    command: ["verdaccio", "--config", "/verdaccio/conf/config.yaml", "--listen", "http://172.17.0.1:4873"]
    volumes:
      - ./config.yaml:/verdaccio/conf/config.yaml:ro
      - verdaccio-storage:/verdaccio/storage

volumes:
  verdaccio-storage:
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/npm-cache/docker-compose.yml"

echo "files written to $R: 4"
bash "$R/install/npm-cache.sh" </dev/null
INSTALL
````

<a id="shared-cache"></a>
## Shared cache /srv/ci-cache

Needs a registered runner. Mounts `/srv/ci-cache` into every job for npm and Sonar. Trusted projects only.

````bash
sudo bash <<'INSTALL'
set -Eeuo pipefail
R=/root/server-init-config

install -d "$R/install"
cat > "$R/install/lib.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Shared helpers for install/*.sh. Sourced, not executed.
# shellcheck shell=bash

REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
export REPO_ROOT

log() { echo "[$(date +%H:%M:%S)] $*"; }
die() { echo "ERROR: $*" >&2; exit 1; }

require_root() { [[ $EUID -eq 0 ]] || die "run as root"; }

selinux_on() { command -v selinuxenabled >/dev/null 2>&1 && selinuxenabled; }

pkg_install() {
  if command -v dnf >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    dnf -y -q install "$@"
  elif command -v apt-get >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    apt-get update -q && apt-get install -y -q "$@"
  else
    die "no supported package manager"
  fi
}

# Label a directory so containers may read files in it (no-op without SELinux).
selinux_label_dir() {
  local dir="$1"
  install -d -m 0755 "$dir"
  selinux_on || return 0
  command -v semanage >/dev/null 2>&1 || pkg_install policycoreutils-python-utils
  # Read the whole list first: grep -q exits at the first match and would SIGPIPE the producer under pipefail.
  if ! grep -q "^${dir}(/\.\*)?" <<<"$(semanage fcontext -l)"; then
    semanage fcontext -a -t container_file_t "${dir}(/.*)?"
  fi
  restorecon -R "$dir"
}

restorecon_if() { if command -v restorecon >/dev/null 2>&1; then restorecon "$@" || true; fi; }

# RHEL-family sudo resets PATH to secure_path, which can leave out /usr/local/bin (so `sudo health` fails).
# Adds a validated drop-in unless some sudoers file already sets a secure_path that includes it.
ensure_secure_path() {
  local f=/etc/sudoers.d/50-secure-path tmp
  command -v visudo >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 0
  if grep -rhsE '^[[:space:]]*Defaults[[:space:]]+secure_path[[:space:]]*=.*/usr/local/bin' /etc/sudoers /etc/sudoers.d 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then
    return 0
  fi
  if ! grep -rhsE '^[[:space:]]*Defaults[[:space:]]+secure_path' /etc/sudoers /etc/sudoers.d 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then
    return 0   # no secure_path at all (Debian family keeps it in sudoers; others use the caller PATH)
  fi
  tmp=$(mktemp)
  printf 'Defaults secure_path="/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin"\n' > "$tmp"
  if visudo -cqf "$tmp"; then
    install -d -m 0750 /etc/sudoers.d
    install -m 0440 -o root -g root "$tmp" "$f"
    log "sudo secure_path now includes /usr/local/bin (${f})"
  else
    echo "WARN: generated sudoers drop-in is invalid, secure_path left as it was" >&2
  fi
  rm -f "$tmp"
}

ci_jobs_running() { docker ps -q --filter "label=com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed=true" 2>/dev/null | wc -l; }

require_runner_idle() {
  local n
  n=$(ci_jobs_running)
  (( n == 0 )) || die "${n} CI job container(s) running, retry when the runner is idle"
}

backup() { cp -a "$1" "${1}.bak.$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)"; }

# Prometheus scrape jobs for the logging stack, shared by install/logging.sh and install/update.sh.
prom_jobs_logging() {
  cat <<'YAML'
  - job_name: loki
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:3100"]
  - job_name: alloy
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:12345"]
YAML
}

# Merge KEY=VALUE entries into the single [[runners]] environment line of config.toml.
runner_env_merge() {
  local cfg=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml existing list key e line
  [[ $(grep -c '^\[\[runners\]\]' "$cfg") -eq 1 ]] || die "expected exactly one [[runners]] block in $cfg"
  existing=$(grep -E '^  environment = \[' "$cfg" | sed -E 's/^  environment = \[(.*)\]$/\1/' || true)
  list="$existing"
  for e in "$@"; do
    key="${e%%=*}"
    if grep -q "\"${key}=" <<<"$list"; then
      list=$(sed -E "s|\"${key}=[^\"]*\"|\"${e}\"|" <<<"$list")
    else
      list="${list:+${list}, }\"${e}\""
    fi
  done
  line="  environment = [${list}]"
  if grep -qE '^  environment = \[' "$cfg"; then
    sed -i -E "s|^  environment = \[.*\]$|${line}|" "$cfg"
  else
    sed -i -E "0,/^  executor = .*/s||${line}\n&|" "$cfg"
  fi
  chmod 600 "$cfg"
  runner_cfg_owner
}

# When the runner service runs as gitlab-runner (install/users.sh), it must own its config after root edits it.
runner_cfg_owner() {
  local cfg=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml
  if [[ -f /etc/systemd/system/gitlab-runner.service.d/10-service-user.conf ]] && id gitlab-runner >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    chown -R gitlab-runner:gitlab-runner /etc/gitlab-runner
    chmod 600 "$cfg"
  fi
}

# --- service user for the monitoring and logging stacks (created by install/users.sh) ---------------
MON_USER="${MON_USER:-monitoring}"

# Set MON_UID and MON_GID, or stop if the service user is missing.
mon_ids() {
  id "$MON_USER" >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "service user ${MON_USER} missing, run install/users.sh first"
  MON_UID=$(id -u "$MON_USER")
  MON_GID=$(id -g "$MON_USER")
}

# env_set FILE KEY VALUE: set KEY=VALUE in a root-only env file.
env_set() {
  local f="$1" k="$2" v="$3"
  touch "$f"
  chmod 600 "$f"
  if grep -q "^${k}=" "$f"; then
    sed -i "s|^${k}=.*|${k}=${v}|" "$f"
  else
    printf '%s=%s\n' "$k" "$v" >> "$f"
  fi
}

# stack_ids DIR [logging]: write the ids the compose file needs into DIR/.env.
stack_ids() {
  local dir="$1" docker_gid journal_gid
  mon_ids
  env_set "${dir}/.env" MON_UID "$MON_UID"
  env_set "${dir}/.env" MON_GID "$MON_GID"
  if [[ "${2:-}" == logging ]]; then
    docker_gid=$(getent group docker | cut -d: -f3)
    [[ -n "$docker_gid" ]] || die "docker group missing"
    env_set "${dir}/.env" DOCKER_GID "$docker_gid"
    journal_gid=$(getent group systemd-journal | cut -d: -f3)
    [[ -n "$journal_gid" ]] || die "systemd-journal group missing"
    # compose rejects equal group_add items, so the journal group is only added when it differs from docker's.
    if [[ "$journal_gid" != "$docker_gid" ]]; then
      printf 'services:\n  alloy:\n    group_add:\n      - "%s"\n' "$journal_gid" > "${dir}/docker-compose.override.yml"
    else
      rm -f "${dir}/docker-compose.override.yml"
    fi
  fi
}

# Config readable by the service user, writable by root only; .env (secrets) stays root-only.
own_stack_dir() {
  local dir="$1"
  chown -R "root:${MON_USER}" "$dir"
  chmod -R u=rwX,g=rX,o= "$dir"
  if [[ -f "${dir}/.env" ]]; then
    chown root:root "${dir}/.env"
    chmod 600 "${dir}/.env"
  fi
}

# Create the stack's containers and volumes without starting them, then hand the volumes to the service user.
chown_volumes() {
  local dir="$1" project v
  mon_ids
  project=$(cd "$dir" && docker compose config --format json | jq -r .name)
  (cd "$dir" && docker compose up --no-start --quiet-pull >/dev/null 2>&1) || (cd "$dir" && docker compose up --no-start >/dev/null)
  for v in $(docker volume ls -q --filter "label=com.docker.compose.project=${project}"); do
    docker run --rm --network none -v "${v}:/v" alpine:3.20 chown -R "${MON_UID}:${MON_GID}" /v
  done
}
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/install/lib.sh"

install -d "$R/install"
cat > "$R/install/shared-cache.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Shared host cache /srv/ci-cache mounted into every job as /ci-cache (npm tarballs, Sonar plugins).
# The cache is shared across all projects on this runner: only use it for trusted projects.
set -Eeuo pipefail
# shellcheck source=install/lib.sh
source "$(dirname "$0")/lib.sh"

HOST_DIR=/srv/ci-cache
MOUNT=/ci-cache
CFG=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml

if [[ "${1:-}" == "-h" || "${1:-}" == "--help" ]]; then
  echo "Usage: $0  mounts $HOST_DIR into all jobs and sets npm_config_cache and SONAR_USER_HOME"
  exit 0
fi
require_root
if ! { [[ -f "$CFG" ]] && grep -q '^\[\[runners\]\]' "$CFG"; }; then die "runner not registered yet"; fi
require_runner_idle

install -d -m 1777 "$HOST_DIR" "$HOST_DIR/npm" "$HOST_DIR/sonar"
if selinux_on; then
  command -v semanage >/dev/null 2>&1 || pkg_install policycoreutils-python-utils
  grep -q "^${HOST_DIR}(/\.\*)?" <<<"$(semanage fcontext -l)" || semanage fcontext -a -t container_file_t "${HOST_DIR}(/.*)?"
  restorecon -R "$HOST_DIR"
fi

backup "$CFG"
if ! grep -q "${HOST_DIR}:${MOUNT}" "$CFG"; then
  grep -qE '^    volumes = \["/cache"\]' "$CFG" || die "unexpected volumes line in [runners.docker], edit manually"
  sed -i -E "s|^    volumes = \[\"/cache\"\]|    volumes = [\"/cache\", \"${HOST_DIR}:${MOUNT}:rw\"]|" "$CFG"
fi
runner_env_merge "npm_config_cache=${MOUNT}/npm" "SONAR_USER_HOME=${MOUNT}/sonar"
systemctl restart gitlab-runner
sleep 3

docker run --rm --user 1000:1000 -v "${HOST_DIR}:${MOUNT}:rw" alpine:3.20 \
  sh -c "touch ${MOUNT}/npm/.probe && rm ${MOUNT}/npm/.probe && echo 'non-root write OK'" \
  || die "containers cannot write to ${HOST_DIR}"

grep -nE 'volumes =|environment =' "$CFG"
gitlab-runner verify 2>&1 | grep -E 'is valid|is not valid' || true
log "OK: shared cache ${HOST_DIR} mounted as ${MOUNT} in all jobs"
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/install/shared-cache.sh"

echo "files written to $R: 2"
bash "$R/install/shared-cache.sh" </dev/null
INSTALL
````

<a id="prewarm"></a>
## Image prewarm

Asks for the path of one image (copy icon in Container Registry) and a deploy token with `read_registry`.

````bash
sudo bash <<'INSTALL'
set -Eeuo pipefail
R=/root/server-init-config

install -d "$R/install"
cat > "$R/install/lib.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Shared helpers for install/*.sh. Sourced, not executed.
# shellcheck shell=bash

REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"

log() { echo "[$(date +%H:%M:%S)] $*"; }
die() { echo "ERROR: $*" >&2; exit 1; }

require_root() { [[ $EUID -eq 0 ]] || die "run as root"; }

selinux_on() { command -v selinuxenabled >/dev/null 2>&1 && selinuxenabled; }

pkg_install() {

  elif command -v apt-get >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    apt-get update -q && apt-get install -y -q "$@"
  else
    die "no supported package manager"
  fi
}

# Label a directory so containers may read files in it (no-op without SELinux).

  local dir="$1"

  selinux_on || return 0

}

restorecon_if() { if command -v restorecon >/dev/null 2>&1; then restorecon "$@" || true; fi; }

  command -v visudo >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 0

    return 0
  fi
  if ! grep -rhsE '^[[:space:]]*Defaults[[:space:]]+secure_path' /etc/sudoers /etc/sudoers.d 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then
    return 0   # no secure_path at all (Debian family keeps it in sudoers; others use the caller PATH)
  fi
  tmp=$(mktemp)
  printf 'Defaults secure_path="/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin"\n' > "$tmp"
  if visudo -cqf "$tmp"; then
    install -d -m 0750 /etc/sudoers.d
    install -m 0440 -o root -g root "$tmp" "$f"
    log "sudo secure_path now includes /usr/local/bin (${f})"
  else
    echo "WARN: generated sudoers drop-in is invalid, secure_path left as it was" >&2
  fi
  rm -f "$tmp"
}

ci_jobs_running() { docker ps -q --filter "label=com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed=true" 2>/dev/null | wc -l; }

require_runner_idle() {
  local n
  n=$(ci_jobs_running)
  (( n == 0 )) || die "${n} CI job container(s) running, retry when the runner is idle"
}

backup() { cp -a "$1" "${1}.bak.$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)"; }

# Prometheus scrape jobs for the logging stack, shared by install/logging.sh and install/update.sh.
prom_jobs_logging() {
  cat <<'YAML'
  - job_name: loki
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:3100"]
  - job_name: alloy
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:12345"]
YAML
}

# Merge KEY=VALUE entries into the single [[runners]] environment line of config.toml.
runner_env_merge() {
  local cfg=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml existing list key e line
  [[ $(grep -c '^\[\[runners\]\]' "$cfg") -eq 1 ]] || die "expected exactly one [[runners]] block in $cfg"
  existing=$(grep -E '^  environment = \[' "$cfg" | sed -E 's/^  environment = \[(.*)\]$/\1/' || true)
  list="$existing"
  for e in "$@"; do
    key="${e%%=*}"
    if grep -q "\"${key}=" <<<"$list"; then
      list=$(sed -E "s|\"${key}=[^\"]*\"|\"${e}\"|" <<<"$list")
    else
      list="${list:+${list}, }\"${e}\""
    fi
  done
  line="  environment = [${list}]"
  if grep -qE '^  environment = \[' "$cfg"; then
    sed -i -E "s|^  environment = \[.*\]$|${line}|" "$cfg"
  else
    sed -i -E "0,/^  executor = .*/s||${line}\n&|" "$cfg"
  fi
  chmod 600 "$cfg"
  runner_cfg_owner
}

# When the runner service runs as gitlab-runner (install/users.sh), it must own its config after root edits it.
runner_cfg_owner() {
  local cfg=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml
  if [[ -f /etc/systemd/system/gitlab-runner.service.d/10-service-user.conf ]] && id gitlab-runner >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    chown -R gitlab-runner:gitlab-runner /etc/gitlab-runner
    chmod 600 "$cfg"
  fi
}

# --- service user for the monitoring and logging stacks (created by install/users.sh) ---------------
MON_USER="${MON_USER:-monitoring}"

# Set MON_UID and MON_GID, or stop if the service user is missing.
mon_ids() {
  id "$MON_USER" >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "service user ${MON_USER} missing, run install/users.sh first"
  MON_UID=$(id -u "$MON_USER")
  MON_GID=$(id -g "$MON_USER")
}

# env_set FILE KEY VALUE: set KEY=VALUE in a root-only env file.
env_set() {
  local f="$1" k="$2" v="$3"
  touch "$f"
  chmod 600 "$f"
  if grep -q "^${k}=" "$f"; then
    sed -i "s|^${k}=.*|${k}=${v}|" "$f"
  else
    printf '%s=%s\n' "$k" "$v" >> "$f"
  fi
}

# stack_ids DIR [logging]: write the ids the compose file needs into DIR/.env.
stack_ids() {
  local dir="$1" docker_gid journal_gid
  mon_ids
  env_set "${dir}/.env" MON_UID "$MON_UID"
  env_set "${dir}/.env" MON_GID "$MON_GID"
  if [[ "${2:-}" == logging ]]; then
    docker_gid=$(getent group docker | cut -d: -f3)
    [[ -n "$docker_gid" ]] || die "docker group missing"
    env_set "${dir}/.env" DOCKER_GID "$docker_gid"
    journal_gid=$(getent group systemd-journal | cut -d: -f3)
    [[ -n "$journal_gid" ]] || die "systemd-journal group missing"
    # compose rejects equal group_add items, so the journal group is only added when it differs from docker's.
    if [[ "$journal_gid" != "$docker_gid" ]]; then
      printf 'services:\n  alloy:\n    group_add:\n      - "%s"\n' "$journal_gid" > "${dir}/docker-compose.override.yml"
    else
      rm -f "${dir}/docker-compose.override.yml"
    fi
  fi
}

# Config readable by the service user, writable by root only; .env (secrets) stays root-only.
own_stack_dir() {
  local dir="$1"
  chown -R "root:${MON_USER}" "$dir"
  chmod -R u=rwX,g=rX,o= "$dir"
  if [[ -f "${dir}/.env" ]]; then
    chown root:root "${dir}/.env"
    chmod 600 "${dir}/.env"
  fi
}

# Create the stack's containers and volumes without starting them, then hand the volumes to the service user.
chown_volumes() {
  local dir="$1" project v
  mon_ids
  project=$(cd "$dir" && docker compose config --format json | jq -r .name)
  (cd "$dir" && docker compose up --no-start --quiet-pull >/dev/null 2>&1) || (cd "$dir" && docker compose up --no-start >/dev/null)
  for v in $(docker volume ls -q --filter "label=com.docker.compose.project=${project}"); do
    docker run --rm --network none -v "${v}:/v" alpine:3.20 chown -R "${MON_UID}:${MON_GID}" /v
  done
}
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/install/lib.sh"

install -d "$R/install"
cat > "$R/install/image-prewarm.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Daily pre-pull of all tags of the CI images in a GitLab Container Registry.
# Needs a project deploy token with read_registry scope.
set -Eeuo pipefail
# shellcheck source=install/lib.sh
source "$(dirname "$0")/lib.sh"

CRANE_VERSION="${CRANE_VERSION:-v0.20.2}"
CONF=/etc/ci-prewarm.conf
REPOS="${REPOS:-mirror/alpine mirror/node mirror/playwright mirror/snyk mirror/sonar-scanner-cli docker}"

if [[ "${1:-}" == "-h" || "${1:-}" == "--help" ]]; then
  cat <<USAGE
Usage: $0
Asks for the full path of one image (copy icon in Container Registry) and a deploy token.
Env: REPOS (space separated, relative to the project registry), CRANE_VERSION
USAGE
  exit 0
fi
require_root

case "$(uname -m)" in
  aarch64) arch=arm64 ;;
  x86_64)  arch=x86_64 ;;
  *) die "unsupported arch $(uname -m)" ;;
esac

if ! command -v crane >/dev/null; then
  log "installing crane ${CRANE_VERSION}"
  tmp=$(mktemp -d)
  base="https://github.com/google/go-containerregistry/releases/download/${CRANE_VERSION}"
  tarball="go-containerregistry_Linux_${arch}.tar.gz"
  curl -fsSL -o "$tmp/$tarball" "$base/$tarball"
  curl -fsSL -o "$tmp/checksums.txt" "$base/checksums.txt"
  (cd "$tmp" && grep " ${tarball}\$" checksums.txt | sha256sum -c -) || { rm -rf "$tmp"; die "crane checksum mismatch"; }
  tar -xzf "$tmp/$tarball" -C "$tmp" crane
  install -m 0755 "$tmp/crane" /usr/local/bin/crane
  rm -rf "$tmp"
  restorecon_if /usr/local/bin/crane
fi

read -ra repo_list <<<"$REPOS"
[[ ${#repo_list[@]} -gt 0 ]] || die "REPOS is empty"
first_repo="${repo_list[0]}"
read -rp "Paste the full path of ${first_repo} (copy icon in Container Registry): " ref
ref="${ref%%:*}"
[[ "$ref" == registry.gitlab.com/*/"${first_repo}" ]] || die "expected registry.gitlab.com/.../${first_repo}, got: $ref"
BASE="${ref%/"${first_repo}"}"
# /etc/ci-prewarm.conf is sourced as root: accept only plain registry path characters.
path_re='^[A-Za-z0-9._/-]+$'
[[ "$BASE" =~ $path_re ]] || die "registry path contains unexpected characters: $BASE"
for r in "${repo_list[@]}"; do [[ "$r" =~ $path_re ]] || die "REPOS entry contains unexpected characters: $r"; done

read -rp "Deploy token username (gitlab+deploy-token-...): " dt_user
read -rsp "Deploy token: " dt_token; echo
[[ "$dt_user" == gitlab+deploy-token-* ]] || die "unexpected deploy token username"
printf '%s' "$dt_token" | docker login registry.gitlab.com -u "$dt_user" --password-stdin >/dev/null
unset dt_token
chmod 600 /root/.docker/config.json
crane ls "$BASE/$first_repo" >/dev/null || die "cannot list $BASE/$first_repo with this token"

{
  printf 'BASE=%q\n' "$BASE"
  printf 'REPOS=(%s)\n' "$(printf '%q ' "${repo_list[@]}")"
} > "$CONF"
chmod 644 "$CONF"

install -m 0755 "$REPO_ROOT/scripts/ci-image-prewarm.sh" /usr/local/bin/ci-image-prewarm.sh
restorecon_if /usr/local/bin/ci-image-prewarm.sh
for u in ci-image-prewarm.service ci-image-prewarm.timer; do
  install -m 0644 "$REPO_ROOT/systemd/$u" "/etc/systemd/system/$u"
  restorecon_if "/etc/systemd/system/$u"
done
systemctl daemon-reload
systemctl enable --now ci-image-prewarm.timer

log "first prewarm run (playwright images are large, this can take minutes)"
systemctl start ci-image-prewarm.service || true
journalctl -t ci-image-prewarm --since "-30min" --no-pager -o cat | grep -E 'OK|NO ARM|FAIL|summary' | sed -E 's/^[0-9T:-]+ //'
log "NO ARM lines: re-mirror those tags with crane copy (see ci/mirror-images.yml)"
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/install/image-prewarm.sh"

install -d "$R/scripts"
cat > "$R/scripts/ci-image-prewarm.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Pre-pull every tag of the CI images listed in /etc/ci-prewarm.conf so jobs start from local images.
set -Eeuo pipefail

CONF="${CONF:-/etc/ci-prewarm.conf}"
log() { logger -t ci-image-prewarm -- "$*"; echo "$(date +%FT%T) $*"; }

if [[ "${1:-}" == "-h" || "${1:-}" == "--help" ]]; then
  echo "Usage: $0  (pulls all tags of REPOS under BASE from $CONF)"
  exit 0
fi
[[ $EUID -eq 0 ]] || { echo "run as root" >&2; exit 1; }
command -v crane >/dev/null 2>&1 || { log "ERROR: crane not installed"; exit 1; }
[[ -f "$CONF" ]] || { log "ERROR: $CONF not found"; exit 1; }

BASE=""
REPOS=()
# shellcheck disable=SC1090
source "$CONF"
[[ -n "$BASE" && ${#REPOS[@]} -gt 0 ]] || { log "ERROR: BASE or REPOS empty in $CONF"; exit 1; }

exec 9>/run/ci-image-prewarm.lock
flock -n 9 || { log "another prewarm is running, exit"; exit 0; }

ok=0; noarm=0; fail=0
for repo in "${REPOS[@]}"; do
  if ! tags=$(crane ls "${BASE}/${repo}" 2>&1); then
    log "FAIL    ${BASE}/${repo}: cannot list tags: ${tags}"
    fail=$((fail + 1))
    continue
  fi
  for tag in $tags; do
    ref="${BASE}/${repo}:${tag}"
    if out=$(docker pull -q "$ref" 2>&1); then
      log "OK      $ref"
      ok=$((ok + 1))
    elif grep -qiE 'no matching manifest|does not match the specified platform' <<<"$out"; then
      log "NO ARM  $ref"
      noarm=$((noarm + 1))
    else
      log "FAIL    $ref: $out"
      fail=$((fail + 1))
    fi
  done
done

log "summary: ok=${ok} no_arm=${noarm} failed=${fail}, disk: $(df -h --output=pcent / | tail -1 | xargs)"
(( fail == 0 ))
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/scripts/ci-image-prewarm.sh"

install -d "$R/systemd"
cat > "$R/systemd/ci-image-prewarm.service" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
[Unit]
Description=Pre-pull CI images from GitLab Registry
After=docker.service network-online.target
Wants=network-online.target

[Service]
Type=oneshot
ExecStart=/usr/local/bin/ci-image-prewarm.sh
Nice=10
IOSchedulingClass=idle
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/systemd/ci-image-prewarm.service"

install -d "$R/systemd"
cat > "$R/systemd/ci-image-prewarm.timer" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
[Unit]
Description=Pre-pull CI images daily and after boot

[Timer]
OnBootSec=5min
OnCalendar=*-*-* 04:15:00
Persistent=true

[Install]
WantedBy=timers.target
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/systemd/ci-image-prewarm.timer"

echo "files written to $R: 5"
INSTALL
sudo bash /root/server-init-config/install/image-prewarm.sh
````

<a id="grafana-domain"></a>
## Grafana on a domain (HTTPS via Caddy)

First: A record to the server IP, ports 80/443 open in the Hetzner Firewall. Replace `grafana.example.com` and `you@example.com` on the last line with your domain and email.

````bash
sudo bash <<'INSTALL'
set -Eeuo pipefail
R=/root/server-init-config

install -d "$R/install"
cat > "$R/install/lib.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Shared helpers for install/*.sh. Sourced, not executed.
# shellcheck shell=bash

REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
export REPO_ROOT

log() { echo "[$(date +%H:%M:%S)] $*"; }
die() { echo "ERROR: $*" >&2; exit 1; }

require_root() { [[ $EUID -eq 0 ]] || die "run as root"; }

selinux_on() { command -v selinuxenabled >/dev/null 2>&1 && selinuxenabled; }

pkg_install() {
  if command -v dnf >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    dnf -y -q install "$@"
  elif command -v apt-get >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    apt-get update -q && apt-get install -y -q "$@"
  else
    die "no supported package manager"
  fi
}

# Label a directory so containers may read files in it (no-op without SELinux).
selinux_label_dir() {
  local dir="$1"
  install -d -m 0755 "$dir"
  selinux_on || return 0
  command -v semanage >/dev/null 2>&1 || pkg_install policycoreutils-python-utils
  # Read the whole list first: grep -q exits at the first match and would SIGPIPE the producer under pipefail.
  if ! grep -q "^${dir}(/\.\*)?" <<<"$(semanage fcontext -l)"; then
    semanage fcontext -a -t container_file_t "${dir}(/.*)?"
  fi
  restorecon -R "$dir"
}

restorecon_if() { if command -v restorecon >/dev/null 2>&1; then restorecon "$@" || true; fi; }

# RHEL-family sudo resets PATH to secure_path, which can leave out /usr/local/bin (so `sudo health` fails).
# Adds a validated drop-in unless some sudoers file already sets a secure_path that includes it.
ensure_secure_path() {
  local f=/etc/sudoers.d/50-secure-path tmp
  command -v visudo >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 0
  if grep -rhsE '^[[:space:]]*Defaults[[:space:]]+secure_path[[:space:]]*=.*/usr/local/bin' /etc/sudoers /etc/sudoers.d 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then
    return 0
  fi
  if ! grep -rhsE '^[[:space:]]*Defaults[[:space:]]+secure_path' /etc/sudoers /etc/sudoers.d 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then
    return 0   # no secure_path at all (Debian family keeps it in sudoers; others use the caller PATH)
  fi
  tmp=$(mktemp)
  printf 'Defaults secure_path="/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin"\n' > "$tmp"
  if visudo -cqf "$tmp"; then
    install -d -m 0750 /etc/sudoers.d
    install -m 0440 -o root -g root "$tmp" "$f"
    log "sudo secure_path now includes /usr/local/bin (${f})"
  else
    echo "WARN: generated sudoers drop-in is invalid, secure_path left as it was" >&2
  fi
  rm -f "$tmp"
}

ci_jobs_running() { docker ps -q --filter "label=com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed=true" 2>/dev/null | wc -l; }

require_runner_idle() {
  local n
  n=$(ci_jobs_running)
  (( n == 0 )) || die "${n} CI job container(s) running, retry when the runner is idle"
}

backup() { cp -a "$1" "${1}.bak.$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)"; }

# Prometheus scrape jobs for the logging stack, shared by install/logging.sh and install/update.sh.
prom_jobs_logging() {
  cat <<'YAML'
  - job_name: loki
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:3100"]
  - job_name: alloy
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:12345"]
YAML
}

# Merge KEY=VALUE entries into the single [[runners]] environment line of config.toml.
runner_env_merge() {
  local cfg=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml existing list key e line
  [[ $(grep -c '^\[\[runners\]\]' "$cfg") -eq 1 ]] || die "expected exactly one [[runners]] block in $cfg"
  existing=$(grep -E '^  environment = \[' "$cfg" | sed -E 's/^  environment = \[(.*)\]$/\1/' || true)
  list="$existing"
  for e in "$@"; do
    key="${e%%=*}"
    if grep -q "\"${key}=" <<<"$list"; then
      list=$(sed -E "s|\"${key}=[^\"]*\"|\"${e}\"|" <<<"$list")
    else
      list="${list:+${list}, }\"${e}\""
    fi
  done
  line="  environment = [${list}]"
  if grep -qE '^  environment = \[' "$cfg"; then
    sed -i -E "s|^  environment = \[.*\]$|${line}|" "$cfg"
  else
    sed -i -E "0,/^  executor = .*/s||${line}\n&|" "$cfg"
  fi
  chmod 600 "$cfg"
  runner_cfg_owner
}

# When the runner service runs as gitlab-runner (install/users.sh), it must own its config after root edits it.
runner_cfg_owner() {
  local cfg=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml
  if [[ -f /etc/systemd/system/gitlab-runner.service.d/10-service-user.conf ]] && id gitlab-runner >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    chown -R gitlab-runner:gitlab-runner /etc/gitlab-runner
    chmod 600 "$cfg"
  fi
}

# --- service user for the monitoring and logging stacks (created by install/users.sh) ---------------
MON_USER="${MON_USER:-monitoring}"

# Set MON_UID and MON_GID, or stop if the service user is missing.
mon_ids() {
  id "$MON_USER" >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "service user ${MON_USER} missing, run install/users.sh first"
  MON_UID=$(id -u "$MON_USER")
  MON_GID=$(id -g "$MON_USER")
}

# env_set FILE KEY VALUE: set KEY=VALUE in a root-only env file.
env_set() {
  local f="$1" k="$2" v="$3"
  touch "$f"
  chmod 600 "$f"
  if grep -q "^${k}=" "$f"; then
    sed -i "s|^${k}=.*|${k}=${v}|" "$f"
  else
    printf '%s=%s\n' "$k" "$v" >> "$f"
  fi
}

# stack_ids DIR [logging]: write the ids the compose file needs into DIR/.env.
stack_ids() {
  local dir="$1" docker_gid journal_gid
  mon_ids
  env_set "${dir}/.env" MON_UID "$MON_UID"
  env_set "${dir}/.env" MON_GID "$MON_GID"
  if [[ "${2:-}" == logging ]]; then
    docker_gid=$(getent group docker | cut -d: -f3)
    [[ -n "$docker_gid" ]] || die "docker group missing"
    env_set "${dir}/.env" DOCKER_GID "$docker_gid"
    journal_gid=$(getent group systemd-journal | cut -d: -f3)
    [[ -n "$journal_gid" ]] || die "systemd-journal group missing"
    # compose rejects equal group_add items, so the journal group is only added when it differs from docker's.
    if [[ "$journal_gid" != "$docker_gid" ]]; then
      printf 'services:\n  alloy:\n    group_add:\n      - "%s"\n' "$journal_gid" > "${dir}/docker-compose.override.yml"
    else
      rm -f "${dir}/docker-compose.override.yml"
    fi
  fi
}

# Config readable by the service user, writable by root only; .env (secrets) stays root-only.
own_stack_dir() {
  local dir="$1"
  chown -R "root:${MON_USER}" "$dir"
  chmod -R u=rwX,g=rX,o= "$dir"
  if [[ -f "${dir}/.env" ]]; then
    chown root:root "${dir}/.env"
    chmod 600 "${dir}/.env"
  fi
}

# Create the stack's containers and volumes without starting them, then hand the volumes to the service user.
chown_volumes() {
  local dir="$1" project v
  mon_ids
  project=$(cd "$dir" && docker compose config --format json | jq -r .name)
  (cd "$dir" && docker compose up --no-start --quiet-pull >/dev/null 2>&1) || (cd "$dir" && docker compose up --no-start >/dev/null)
  for v in $(docker volume ls -q --filter "label=com.docker.compose.project=${project}"); do
    docker run --rm --network none -v "${v}:/v" alpine:3.20 chown -R "${MON_UID}:${MON_GID}" /v
  done
}
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/install/lib.sh"

install -d "$R/install"
cat > "$R/install/grafana-domain.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Publish Grafana on an HTTPS domain via Caddy (automatic Let's Encrypt).
# Requires: monitoring installed, A record pointing to this server (DNS only, no proxy), ports 80/443 open.
set -Eeuo pipefail
# shellcheck source=install/lib.sh
source "$(dirname "$0")/lib.sh"

DOMAIN="${DOMAIN:-}"
ACME_EMAIL="${ACME_EMAIL:-}"
CADDY_IMAGE="${CADDY_IMAGE:-caddy:2.10.0}"
DIR=/opt/monitoring

if [[ "${1:-}" == "-h" || "${1:-}" == "--help" || -z "$DOMAIN" || -z "$ACME_EMAIL" ]]; then
  echo "Usage: DOMAIN=grafana.example.com ACME_EMAIL=you@example.com $0"
  [[ "${1:-}" == "-h" || "${1:-}" == "--help" ]] && exit 0
  exit 1
fi
require_root
[[ -f "$DIR/docker-compose.yml" && -f "$DIR/.env" ]] || die "monitoring stack not found in $DIR"
command -v openssl >/dev/null || pkg_install openssl

server_ip=$(curl -fsS --max-time 5 http://169.254.169.254/hetzner/v1/metadata/public-ipv4 2>/dev/null || curl -4 -fsS --max-time 5 https://ifconfig.me)
dns_ip=$(getent ahostsv4 "$DOMAIN" | awk 'NR==1 { print $1 }')
log "server ip: $server_ip, dns ip: ${dns_ip:-<none>}"
[[ -n "$dns_ip" ]] || die "$DOMAIN does not resolve yet"
[[ "$dns_ip" == "$server_ip" ]] || die "$DOMAIN points to $dns_ip, expected $server_ip (DNS proxy enabled?)"
if ss -ltn | awk '$4 ~ /:(80|443)$/' | grep -q .; then die "ports 80/443 already in use"; fi

backup "$DIR/.env"
set_env() {
  if grep -q "^$1=" "$DIR/.env"; then sed -i "s|^$1=.*|$1=$2|" "$DIR/.env"; else echo "$1=$2" >> "$DIR/.env"; fi
}
set_env GF_SERVER_DOMAIN "$DOMAIN"
set_env GF_SERVER_ROOT_URL "https://${DOMAIN}/"
set_env GF_SECURITY_COOKIE_SECURE "true"
set_env GF_SECURITY_COOKIE_SAMESITE "lax"
set_env GF_SECURITY_DISABLE_GRAVATAR "true"
set_env GF_SECURITY_CONTENT_SECURITY_POLICY "true"
chmod 600 "$DIR/.env"

install -d -m 0755 "$DIR/caddy"
sed -e "s|__DOMAIN__|${DOMAIN}|g" -e "s|__ACME_EMAIL__|${ACME_EMAIL}|g" \
  "$REPO_ROOT/monitoring/caddy/Caddyfile.tmpl" > "$DIR/caddy/Caddyfile"

cat > "$DIR/docker-compose.override.yml" <<YAML
services:
  caddy:
    image: ${CADDY_IMAGE}
    restart: unless-stopped
    network_mode: host
    mem_limit: 128m
    cap_add:
      - NET_BIND_SERVICE
    volumes:
      - ./caddy/Caddyfile:/etc/caddy/Caddyfile:ro
      - caddy-data:/data
      - caddy-config:/config
    depends_on: [grafana]

volumes:
  caddy-data:
  caddy-config:
YAML
restorecon_if -R "$DIR"

docker run --rm -v "$DIR/caddy/Caddyfile:/etc/caddy/Caddyfile:ro" "$CADDY_IMAGE" \
  caddy validate --config /etc/caddy/Caddyfile --adapter caddyfile

cd "$DIR"
docker compose pull -q caddy
docker compose up -d

ok=0
for _ in $(seq 1 40); do
  if curl -fsS --max-time 5 "https://${DOMAIN}/api/health" >/dev/null 2>&1; then ok=1; break; fi
  sleep 3
done
if [[ "$ok" != "1" ]]; then
  docker compose logs caddy --tail 40
  die "https://${DOMAIN} not reachable, check firewall ports 80/443 and DNS"
fi

echo | openssl s_client -connect "${DOMAIN}:443" -servername "$DOMAIN" 2>/dev/null | openssl x509 -noout -issuer -dates
curl -sS -o /dev/null -w "http redirect: %{http_code} -> %{redirect_url}\n" "http://${DOMAIN}/"
exposed=$(ss -ltn | awk '/:(3000|9090|9100|9252) / && $4 !~ /^127\.0\.0\.1:/' | wc -l)
[[ "$exposed" == "0" ]] || die "an internal port is exposed"
log "OK: https://${DOMAIN}"
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 755 "$R/install/grafana-domain.sh"

install -d "$R/monitoring/caddy"
cat > "$R/monitoring/caddy/Caddyfile.tmpl" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
{
	email __ACME_EMAIL__
	admin off
}

__DOMAIN__ {
	encode zstd gzip

	header {
		Strict-Transport-Security "max-age=31536000"
		X-Content-Type-Options "nosniff"
		X-Frame-Options "DENY"
		Referrer-Policy "strict-origin-when-cross-origin"
		-Server
	}

	reverse_proxy 127.0.0.1:3000

	log {
		output stdout
		format console
	}
}
__SIC_EOF__
chmod 644 "$R/monitoring/caddy/Caddyfile.tmpl"

echo "files written to $R: 3"
INSTALL
sudo DOMAIN=grafana.example.com ACME_EMAIL=you@example.com bash /root/server-init-config/install/grafana-domain.sh
````

<a id="selinux"></a>
## SELinux enforcing

Switches SELinux to enforcing now and after reboot, then restarts and checks Docker and the runner. Refuses to run while CI jobs are running.

````bash
sudo bash <<'INSTALL'
set -Eeuo pipefail
R=/root/server-init-config

install -d "$R/install"
cat > "$R/install/lib.sh" <<'__SIC_EOF__'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Shared helpers for install/*.sh. Sourced, not executed.
# shellcheck shell=bash

REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
export REPO_ROOT

log() { echo "[$(date +%H:%M:%S)] $*"; }
die() { echo "ERROR: $*" >&2; exit 1; }

require_root() { [[ $EUID -eq 0 ]] || die "run as root"; }

selinux_on() { command -v selinuxenabled >/dev/null 2>&1 && selinuxenabled; }

pkg_install() {
  if command -v dnf >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    dnf -y -q install "$@"
  elif command -v apt-get >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    apt-get update -q && apt-get install -y -q "$@"
  else
    die "no supported package manager"
  fi
}

# Label a directory so containers may read files in it (no-op without SELinux).
selinux_label_dir() {
  local dir="$1"
  install -d -m 0755 "$dir"
  selinux_on || return 0
  command -v semanage >/dev/null 2>&1 || pkg_install policycoreutils-python-utils
  # Read the whole list first: grep -q exits at the first match and would SIGPIPE the producer under pipefail.
  if ! grep -q "^${dir}(/\.\*)?" <<<"$(semanage fcontext -l)"; then
    semanage fcontext -a -t container_file_t "${dir}(/.*)?"
  fi
  restorecon -R "$dir"
}

restorecon_if() { if command -v restorecon >/dev/null 2>&1; then restorecon "$@" || true; fi; }

# RHEL-family sudo resets PATH to secure_path, which can leave out /usr/local/bin (so `sudo health` fails).
# Adds a validated drop-in unless some sudoers file already sets a secure_path that includes it.
ensure_secure_path() {

    return 0
  fi
  if ! grep -rhsE '^[[:space:]]*Defaults[[:space:]]+secure_path' /etc/sudoers /etc/sudoers.d 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then
    return 0   # no secure_path at all (Debian family keeps it in sudoers; others use the caller PATH)
  fi

  if visudo -cqf "$tmp"; then
    install -d -m 0750 /etc/sudoers.d
    install -m 0440 -o root -g root "$tmp" "$f"
    log "sudo secure_path now includes /usr/local/bin (${f})"
  else
    echo "WARN: generated sudoers drop-in is invalid, secure_path left as it was" >&2
  fi
  rm -f "$tmp"
}

ci_jobs_running() { docker ps -q --filter "label=com.gitlab.gitlab-runner.managed=true" 2>/dev/null | wc -l; }

require_runner_idle() {
  local n
  n=$(ci_jobs_running)
  (( n == 0 )) || die "${n} CI job container(s) running, retry when the runner is idle"
}

backup() { cp -a "$1" "${1}.bak.$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)"; }

# Prometheus scrape jobs for the logging stack, shared by install/logging.sh and install/update.sh.
prom_jobs_logging() {
  cat <<'YAML'
  - job_name: loki
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:3100"]
  - job_name: alloy
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["127.0.0.1:12345"]
YAML
}

# Merge KEY=VALUE entries into the single [[runners]] environment line of config.toml.
runner_env_merge() {
  local cfg=/etc/gitlab-runner/config.toml existing list key e line

  existing=$(grep -E '^  environment = \[' "$cfg" | sed -E 's/^  environment = \[(.*)\]$/\1/' || true)
  list="$existing"

    key="${e%%=*}"

    chown -R gitlab-runner:gitlab-runner /etc/gitlab-runner

}

# --- service user for the monitoring and logging stacks (created by install/users.sh) ---------------
MON_USER="${MON_USER:-monitoring}"

# Set MON_UID and MON_GID, or stop if the service user is missing.
mon_ids() {

  MON_GID=$(id -g "$MON_USER")
}

# env_set FILE KEY VALUE: set KEY=VALUE in a root-only env file.
env_set() {
  local f="$1" k="$2" v="$3"
  touch "$f"
  chmod 600 "$f"

    sed -i "s|^${k}=.*|${k}=${v}|" "$f"
  else
    printf '%s=%s\n' "$k" "$v" >> "$f"
  fi
}

# stack_ids DIR [logging]: write the ids the compose file needs into DIR/.env.
stack_ids() {
  local dir="$1" docker_gid journal_gid
  mon_ids
  env_set "${dir}/.env" MON_UID "$MON_UID"
  env_set "${dir}/.env" MON_GID "$MON_GID"
  if [[ "${2:-}" == logging ]]; then
    docker_gid=$(getent group docker | cut -d: -f3)
    [[ -n "$docker_gid" ]] || die "docker group missing"
    env_set "${dir}/.env" DOCKER_GID "$docker_gid"
    journal_gid=$(getent group systemd-journal | cut -d: -f3)
    [[ -n "$journal_gid" ]] || die "systemd-journal group missing"
    # compose rejects equal group_add items, so the journal group is only added when it differs from docker's.
    if [[ "$journal_gid" != "$docker_gid" ]]; then
      printf 'services:\n  alloy:\n    group_add:\n      - "%s"\n' "$journal_gid" > "${dir}/docker-compose.override.yml"
    else

    fi

}
````
